%LET dtDataBase = %sysfunc(mdy(%substr(%scan(&sysparm.,1,"/"),5,2),%substr(%scan(&sysparm.,1,"/"),7,2),%substr(%scan(&sysparm.,1,"/"),1,4)));

/*Calcula Metricas DV10*/;
%macro r02CalcDV10 (dtDataBase, nVrDolar);
	/*Soma do DV10 por Fator de Risco*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.SOMA_DV10 AS
			SELECT DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				t1.Fator_de_Risco,
/*				t1.MesaOperacao,*/
/*				t1.GrupoDeConciliacao,*/
				(SUM(t1.vrDV10)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
				GROUP BY t1.Fator_de_Risco;
/*				t1.MesaOperacao,*/
/*				,t1.GrupoDeConciliacao;*/
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.SOMA_DV10_Global_AUX AS
			SELECT DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				t1.Fator_de_Risco,
				t1.Modalidade,
				(SUM(t1.vrDV10)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
				GROUP BY
					t1.Fator_de_Risco,
					t1.Modalidade
		;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cupom_IPCA_Consolidado AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupom IPCA - Consolidado' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10 t1
				WHERE t1.Fator_de_Risco = 'Cupom IPCA';
	QUIT;

	%if &nDataBase. >= 20171219 %then %do;

		%if &nDataBase. < &dtCorteLibor. %then %do;
			/* Calculo do Limite de DV10 Cupom Offshore para o periodo de vigencia da Libor	*/
			PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cupom_Offshore_Consolidado AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - Consolidado' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.vrDV10)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM
					_DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE
					 (t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor') AND t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM') OR
					 (t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor') AND t1.MesaOperacao NE '23 - ALM') OR
					  t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex' OR
					  t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
					  t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M';
			QUIT;
		%end;
		%else %do;
			/*	Calculo do Limite de DV10 Cupom Offshore após o tombamento de SOFR e
				descontinuacao da Libor	*/
			PROC SQL;
				CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cupom_Offshore_Consolidado AS
					SELECT 	DISTINCT
						t1.DtReferencia,
						'DV10 Cupons Offshore - Consolidado' as Metrica,
						(SUM(t1.vrDV10)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
					FROM
						_DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
					WHERE
						 t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR','Cupom Offshore')
						;
			QUIT;
		%end;

		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Global_Consolidado AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
/*					MesaOperacao,*/
/*					t1.GrupoDeConciliacao,*/
					'DV10 Total - Consolidado' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_Global_AUX t1
					WHERE
						(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
						 t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial') ;
/*					GROUP BY t1.MesaOperacao,t1.GrupoDeConciliacao;*/
		QUIT;

	%end;
	%ELSE %do;

		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cupom_Offshore_Consolidado AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupom Offshore Consolidado' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10 t1
					WHERE t1.Fator_de_Risco = 'Cupom Offshore' OR t1.Fator_de_Risco = 'Cupom Libor';
		QUIT;

		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Global_Consolidado AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Global Consolidado' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10 t1
					WHERE t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT = 'Spread Titulos Publicos' AND t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial';
		QUIT;

	%end;

	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cupom_USD_Consolidado AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupom USD - Consolidado' as Metrica,
				(t1.ValorMetrica/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10 t1
				WHERE t1.Fator_de_Risco = 'Cupom USD';
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_PreFixado_Consolidado AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Pré-Fixado - Consolidado' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10 t1
				WHERE t1.Fator_de_Risco = 'Pre-Fixado';
	QUIT;

%mend r02CalcDV10;

/*Calcula Metricas DV10*/;
%macro r02CalcDV10_2018 (dtDataBase, nVrDolar, nZero);

	/*Soma do DV10 por Fator de Risco*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 AS
			SELECT   DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				t1.MesaOperacao,
				t1.CarteiraGerencial,
				t1.BookGerencial,
				t1.Modalidade,
				t1.Fator_de_Risco,
/*				t1.Base,*/
/*				t1.GrupoDeConciliacao,*/
				(SUM(t1.vrDV10)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
				GROUP BY t1.DtReferencia,
							t1.MesaOperacao,
							t1.CarteiraGerencial,
							t1.BookGerencial,
							t1.Modalidade,
							t1.Fator_de_Risco
/*							t1.Base,*/
/*							t1.GrupoDeConciliacao*/
				ORDER BY 3;
	QUIT;

	/*=============================================================================================*/
	/* Calculo dos Limites de DV10 por Mesa durante o periodo de vigencia da Libor				   */
	/*=============================================================================================*/
	%if &nDataBase. < &dtCorteLibor. %then %do;

		/*DV10 Total - FICC*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_FICC AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - FICC' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') OR
													(t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
													 t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M')) AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'FICC' OR t1.MesaOperacao = '6 - VOLATILIDADE')
													;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - FICC GESTOR*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_FICC_GESTOR AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - FICC GESTOR' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') OR
													(t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
													 t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M')) AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao = '18 - FICC GESTOR')
													;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - BSM*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - BSM' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') OR
													(t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
													 t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M')) AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS "BSM")
													;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - ALM*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_ALM AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - ALM' as Metrica,
	/*				Fator_de_Risco,*/
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') OR
													(t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
													 t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M')) AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao = "23 - ALM")
													;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - BSM + ALM*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - BSM + ALM' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') OR
													(t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
													 t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M')) AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM' OR t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM')
													;
		QUIT;

		/*===================================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - BSM + FICC*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_FICC AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - BSM + FICC' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') OR
													(t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future' OR
													 t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M')) AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'FICC' OR t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM')
													;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - MESA NÃO INFORMADA*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_MESANINFO AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - MESA NAO INFORMADA' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Cupom Offshore',
													'Spread Titulos Publicos',
													'Cupom IPCA Gerencial',
													'Cupom Libor Gerencial',
													'Cupom USD Gerencial',
													'Pre-Fixado Gerencial') AND
													(t1.MesaOperacao = "X - NAO INFORMADO");
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_FICC AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.vrDV10)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor','Cupom Offshore') AND
							((t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'FICC' or t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'VOLATILIDADE'))) OR
							(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
							t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC GESTOR*/
			PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_GESTOR AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC GESTOR' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor','Cupom Offshore') AND
							t1.MesaOperacao = '18 - FICC GESTOR') OR
							(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
							t1.MesaOperacao = '18 - FICC GESTOR' AND
							t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - ALM*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_ALM AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupons Offshore - ALM' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'ALM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'ALM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - BSM*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_BSM AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupons Offshore - BSM' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future')
			;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - Mesa não Informada*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_MESANINFO AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupons Offshore - MESA NÃO INFORMADA' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
				(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor') AND
						t1.MesaOperacao = 'X - NAO INFORMADO')OR
						(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
						t1.MesaOperacao = 'X - NAO INFORMADO' AND
						t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC OFFSHORE*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_Offshore AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC OFFSHORE' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.vrDV10)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
					WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Libor','Cupom Offshore') AND
 					(t1.MesaOperacao = '9 - FICC OFFSHORE' OR t1.BookGerencial = '488 - DERIVATIVOS LIBOR E HEDGE')) OR
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND t1.MesaOperacao eq '9 - FICC OFFSHORE') OR
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR','Cupom Libor','Cupom Offshore') AND t1.BookGerencial IN ('578 - HEDGE LIBOR LOCAL - MERCADO','616 - HEDGE SWAP LOCAL - MERCADO'))
			;
		QUIT;

	%end;
	%else %do;
	/*=========================================================================================*/
	/* Calculo dos Limites de DV10 por Mesa após o tombamento da SOFR e descontinuacao da Libor*/
	/*=========================================================================================*/

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - FICC*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_FICC AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - FICC' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
					   			t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
					AND
					  (t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'FICC' OR t1.MesaOperacao = '6 - VOLATILIDADE')
			;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - FICC GESTOR*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_FICC_GESTOR AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - FICC GESTOR' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
					   		t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
					 AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao = '18 - FICC GESTOR')
				;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - BSM*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - BSM' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
				   			  t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
				 	AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS "BSM")
				;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - ALM*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_ALM AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - ALM' as Metrica,
	/*				Fator_de_Risco,*/
					ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
				   			  t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
				 	AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao = "23 - ALM")
				;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - BSM + ALM*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - BSM + ALM' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
				   			  t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
				 	AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM' OR t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM')
				;
		QUIT;

		/*===================================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - BSM + FICC*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_FICC AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - BSM + FICC' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
				   			  t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
				 	AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM' OR t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'FICC')
			;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Total - MESA NÃO INFORMADA*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_MESANINFO AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
					'DV10 Total - MESA NAO INFORMADA' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT IN ('Spread Titulos Publicos') AND
			   			  	  t1.Fator_de_Risco not contains 'Gerencial')
				 	AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao = "X - NAO INFORMADO")
				;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_FICC AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.vrDV10)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
					WHERE (t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR','Cupom Offshore') AND
							((t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'FICC' or t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'VOLATILIDADE'))) OR
							(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
							t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC GESTOR*/
			PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_GESTOR AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC GESTOR' as Metrica,
					ValorMetrica
				FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
					WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR','Cupom Offshore') AND
							t1.MesaOperacao = '18 - FICC GESTOR') OR
							(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
							t1.MesaOperacao = '18 - FICC GESTOR' AND
							t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - ALM*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_ALM AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupons Offshore - ALM' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao = '23 - ALM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'ALM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'ALM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - BSM*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_BSM AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupons Offshore - BSM' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '643 - FUTURO DE SOFR 3M') OR

					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom Offshore') AND
					t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'BSM' AND
					t1.Modalidade eq '298 - TBonds Future')
			;
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - Mesa não Informada*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_MESANINFO AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia,
				'DV10 Cupons Offshore - MESA NÃO INFORMADA' as Metrica,
				ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.SOMA_DV10_2018 t1
				WHERE
				(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR') AND
						t1.MesaOperacao = 'X - NAO INFORMADO')OR
						(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND
						t1.MesaOperacao = 'X - NAO INFORMADO' AND
						t1.Modalidade eq '549 - TBonds Future EUX-Eurex');
		QUIT;

		/*=============================================*/
		/*DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC OFFSHORE*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Libor_Offshore AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Cupons Offshore - FICC OFFSHORE' as Metrica,
					(SUM(t1.vrDV10)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM _DV10.FLUXODV10&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
					WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom SOFR','Cupom Offshore') AND
 					(t1.MesaOperacao = '9 - FICC OFFSHORE' OR t1.BookGerencial = '488 - DERIVATIVOS LIBOR E HEDGE')) OR
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR') AND t1.MesaOperacao eq '9 - FICC OFFSHORE') OR
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Cupom EUR','Cupom SOFR','Cupom Offshore') AND t1.BookGerencial IN ('578 - HEDGE LIBOR LOCAL - MERCADO','616 - HEDGE SWAP LOCAL - MERCADO'))
			;
		QUIT;

	%end;

	/*===========================================================*/

	%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.DV10_Total_FICC_GESTOR) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;
			DATA WORK.DV10_Total_FICC_GESTOR ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'DV10 Total - FICC GESTOR';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;
		%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
				PROC SQL;
					CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_FICC_GESTOR AS
						SELECT 	DISTINCT
							t1.DtReferencia,
							t1.Metrica,
							(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
						FROM WORK.DV10_Total_FICC_GESTOR t1;
				QUIT;
		%END;

	%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.DV10_Total_BSM) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;
			DATA WORK.DV10_Total_BSM ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'DV10 Total - BSM';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;
		%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
				PROC SQL;
					CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM AS
						SELECT 	DISTINCT
							t1.DtReferencia,
							t1.Metrica,
							(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
						FROM WORK.DV10_Total_BSM t1;
				QUIT;
		%END;

	%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.DV10_Total_ALM) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;
			DATA WORK.DV10_Total_ALM ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'DV10 Total - ALM';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;
		%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
				PROC SQL;
					CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_ALM AS
						SELECT 	DISTINCT
							t1.DtReferencia,
							t1.Metrica,
							(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
						FROM WORK.DV10_Total_ALM t1;
				QUIT;
		%END;

	%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;
			DATA WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'DV10 Total - BSM + ALM';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;

		%END;

				PROC SQL;
					CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM AS
						SELECT 	DISTINCT
							t1.DtReferencia,

			%END;

	%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.DV10_Total_MESANINFO) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;
			DATA WORK.DV10_Total_MESANINFO ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'DV10 Total - MESA NAO INFORMADA';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;
		%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
				PROC SQL;
					CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_MESANINFO AS
						SELECT 	DISTINCT
							t1.DtReferencia,
							t1.Metrica,
							(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
						FROM WORK.DV10_Total_MESANINFO t1;
				QUIT;
		%END;
/*=============================================*/

	/*DV10 Cp IPCA FICC*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cp_IPCA_FICC AS
			SELECT 	DISTINCT
				t1.DtReferencia FORMAT = yymmdd10.,
				'DV10 Cupom IPCA - FICC' as Metrica,

							(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
						FROM WORK.DV10_Cp_IPCA_GESTOR t1;
				QUIT;
			%END;

	/*DV10 Cp IPCA RENDA FIXA*/

		CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Cp_IPCA_RENDA_FIXA AS

/*=============================================*/

/*=============================================*/
	/*DV10 Total IPCA+IGPM - FICC VOLATILIDADE*/
	PROC SQL;

						WHERE t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'X - NAO INFORMADO' AND
								t1.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Pre-Fixado','Cupom TJLP');

				DATA WORK.DV10_Pre_CpTJLP_MESANINFO ;

					Metrica = 'DV10 Pré + Cupom TJLP - MESA NÃO INFORMADA';
					ValorMetrica = &nZero.;

				PROC SQL;

							t1.DtReferencia,
							t1.Metrica,
							(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica

				QUIT;
			%END;
		/*=============================================*/

		/*=========================================================================*/
		/* Tabela de referencia para o Limite de DV10 Total ALM + BSM Maior 4 anos */
		/*=========================================================================*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.SOMA_DV10_ALOCACAO AS
				SELECT   DISTINCT
					input(t1.Vertice, best12.) as Vertice,
					t1.DtReferencia,
					t1.MesaOperacao,
					t1.CarteiraGerencial,
					t1.BookGerencial,
					t1.Modalidade,
					t1.Fator_de_Risco,
					(SUM(t1.VrDVutilizado)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
				FROM
					_ALOC._ALOCACAO_&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE
					upcase(t1.Filtro) = 'RM'
				GROUP BY
					(CALCULATED Vertice),
					t1.DtReferencia,
					t1.MesaOperacao,
					t1.CarteiraGerencial,
					t1.BookGerencial,
					t1.Modalidade,
					t1.Fator_de_Risco
				ORDER BY
					4
				;
		QUIT;

		/*DV10 Total - ALM + BSM Maior 4 Anos*/
		PROC SQL;
			CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM_MAIOR_4_ANOS AS
				SELECT 	DISTINCT
					t1.DtReferencia,
					'DV10 Total - ALM + BSM Maior 4 Anos' as Metrica,
					t1.ValorMetrica
				FROM
					WORK.SOMA_DV10_ALOCACAO t1
				WHERE
					(t1.Fator_de_Risco NE 'Spread Titulos Publicos' AND
				   	 t1.Fator_de_Risco NOT CONTAINS 'Gerencial')
				 	AND
					(t1.MesaOperacao EQ '23 - ALM' OR t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM')
					AND
					t1.Vertice > 1008

			;
		QUIT;

		%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM_MAIOR_4_ANOS) eq 0 %THEN
			%DO;
				DATA WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM_MAIOR_4_ANOS ;
					DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
					Metrica = 'DV10 Total - ALM + BSM Maior 4 Anos';
					ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
					ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
				RUN;
			%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
			PROC SQL;
				CREATE TABLE WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM_MAIOR_4_ANOS AS
					SELECT 	DISTINCT
						t1.DtReferencia,
						t1.Metrica,
						(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
					FROM
						WORK.DV10_Total_BSM_ALM_MAIOR_4_ANOS t1
				;
			QUIT;
		%END;
		/*=========================================================================*/

%mend r02CalcDV10_2018;

/*Calcula Metricas VAR*/;
%macro r03CalcVaR (dtDataBase, nDataBase);

	/*Calculo do VaR Consolidado*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO AS
			SELECT t1.nuCenario,
				((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500
					GROUP BY 	t1.nuCenario
						ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_CA(DROP=nuCenario);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Consolidado - Limite Superior';
		SET WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO;
		IF _N_=5 then OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_CRC(DROP=nuCenario);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Consolidado - Limite Operacional';
		SET WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO;
		IF _N_=5 then OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	/*Calculo do VaR CONSOLIDADO Bankin/Trading*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_B_T_BASE AS
			SELECT
				t1.TpNegociacao,
				t1.nuCenario,
				((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500
					GROUP BY 	t1.TpNegociacao,
						t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 1,3;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_B_T(DROP=nuCenario TpNegociacao ValorMetricaAUX);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "BANKING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR CONSOLIDADO BANKING';

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "TRADING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR CONSOLIDADO TRADING';
		SET WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_B_T_BASE;

		IF _N_=5 then
			OUTPUT;

		IF _N_=505 then
			OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	/* */
	DATA WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_B_T_OPER(DROP=nuCenario TpNegociacao ValorMetricaAUX);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "BANKING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR CONSOLIDADO BANKING OPERACIONAL';

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "TRADING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR CONSOLIDADO TRADING OPERACIONAL';
		SET WORK.VAR_CONSOLIDADO_B_T_BASE;

		IF _N_=5 then
			OUTPUT;

		IF _N_=505 then
			OUTPUT;
	RUN;

/*	%end;*/

%mend r03CalcVAR;

/*Calcula Metricas VaR por Mesa de Operações*/;
%macro r04CalcVARMesa_2017 (dtDataBase, nDataBase);

/*VaR Mesa RF LOCAL*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_RF_LOCAL AS
			SELECT  t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao eq '2 - RENDA FIXA LOCAL CORP'
					GROUP BY t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_RF_LOCAL(DROP=nuCenario);
		SET WORK.VAR_RF_LOCAL;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nVAR_RF_LOCAL',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.VAR_RF_LOCAL;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Mesa RF LOCAL';
		ValorMetrica = &nVAR_RF_LOCAL.;
	RUN;

	/*VaR Mesa RF ME LOCAL*/
		PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_RF_ME_LOCAL AS
			SELECT  t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao eq '3 - RF ME LOCAL'
					GROUP BY t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_RF_ME_LOCAL(DROP=nuCenario);
		SET WORK.VAR_RF_ME_LOCAL;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nVAR_RF_ME_LOCAL',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.VAR_RF_ME_LOCAL;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Mesa RF ME LOCAL';
		ValorMetrica = &nVAR_RF_ME_LOCAL.;
	RUN;

	/*VaR Mesa RF ME EXTERNA*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_RF_ME_EXTERNA AS
			SELECT  t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao eq '4 - RF ME EXTERNA'
					GROUP BY t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_RF_ME_EXTERNA(DROP=nuCenario);
		SET WORK.VAR_RF_ME_EXTERNA;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nVAR_RF_ME_EXTERNA',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.VAR_RF_ME_EXTERNA;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Mesa RF ME EXTERNA';
		ValorMetrica = (&nVAR_RF_ME_EXTERNA.);
	RUN;

	/*VaR CAIXA*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_CAIXA AS
			SELECT  t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao eq '5 - CAIXA'
					GROUP BY t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_CAIXA(DROP=nuCenario);
		SET WORK.VAR_CAIXA;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nVAR_CAIXA',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.VAR_CAIXA;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Mesa CAIXA';
		ValorMetrica = (&nVAR_CAIXA.);
	RUN;

	/*VaR VOLATILIDADE*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VOLATILIDADE AS
			SELECT  t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao eq '6 - VOLATILIDADE'
					GROUP BY t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VOLATILIDADE(DROP=nuCenario);
		SET WORK.VOLATILIDADE;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nVOLATILIDADE',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.VOLATILIDADE;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR - Mesa VOLATILIDADE';
		ValorMetrica = (&nVOLATILIDADE.);
	RUN;

	/*RENDA FIXA EXTERNA*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.RENDA_FIXA_EXTERNA AS
			SELECT  t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica

			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM'

		/*Calculo do VAT TESOURARIA Total*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_TESOURARIA AS
			SELECT t1.nuCenario,
				((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND t1.MesaOperacao NOT eq '23 - ALM'
					GROUP BY 	t1.nuCenario
						ORDER BY 2;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_TESOURARIA(DROP=nuCenario ValorMetricaAUX);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'VaR Tesouraria';
		SET WORK.VAR_TESOURARIA;

		IF _N_=5 then
			OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	/*Calculo do VaR FICC Bankin/Trading*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_FICC_B_T AS
			SELECT
				t1.TpNegociacao,
				t1.nuCenario,
				((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND (t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'FICC' OR t1.MesaOperacao IN ('6 - VOLATILIDADE','6 - FICC VOLATILIDADE'))
					GROUP BY 	t1.TpNegociacao,
						t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 1,3;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_FICC_B_T(DROP=nuCenario TpNegociacao ValorMetricaAUX);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "BANKING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR FICC BANKING';

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "TRADING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR FICC TRADING';
		SET WORK.VAR_FICC_B_T;

		IF _N_=5 then
			OUTPUT;

		IF _N_=505 then
			OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	/*Calculo do VaR BSM Bankin/Trading*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_BSM_B_T AS
			SELECT
				t1.TpNegociacao,
				t1.nuCenario,
				((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND t1.MesaOperacao CONTAINS 'BSM'
					GROUP BY 	t1.TpNegociacao,
						t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 1,3;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_BSM_B_T(DROP=nuCenario TpNegociacao ValorMetricaAUX);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "BANKING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR BSM BANKING';

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "TRADING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR BSM TRADING';
		SET WORK.VAR_BSM_B_T;

		IF _N_=5 then
			OUTPUT;

		IF _N_=505 then
			OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	/*Calculo do VaR ALM Bankin/Trading*/
	PROC SQL;
		CREATE TABLE WORK.VAR_ALM_B_T AS
			SELECT  t1.TpNegociacao
				,t1.nuCenario
				,((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
				WHERE t1.nuCenario <= 500  AND  t1.MesaOperacao eq '23 - ALM'
					GROUP BY t1.TpNegociacao,
							t1.nuCenario
					ORDER BY 1,3;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.VAR_ALM_B_T(DROP=nuCenario TpNegociacao ValorMetricaAUX);

			Metrica = 'VaR ALM BANKING';

		IF TpNegociacao EQ "TRADING" THEN
			Metrica = 'VaR ALM TRADING';
		SET WORK.VAR_ALM_B_T;

		IF _N_=5 then
			OUTPUT;

		IF _N_=505 then
			OUTPUT;
	RUN;

	/*Calculo do VaR TESOURARIA Bankin/Trading*/
	PROC SQL;

				t1.TpNegociacao,
				t1.nuCenario,
				((sqrt(10))*SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1

	RUN;
%mend r04CalcVARMesa_2018;

/*Calcula Metricas Estresse*/;
%macro r05CalcEstresse (dtDataBase, nDataBase);

	/*Estresse ALM*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_ALM AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.tpCenario
						,(SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Cenario_Subcomissao' AND t1.tpCenario NOT IN ('HistoricoD','HistoricoDerivativos') AND t1.MesaOperacao eq '23 - ALM'
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_ALM;
		SET WORK.Estresse_ALM;
		IF tpCenario='Otimista' then call symput('nEstresse_ALM',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_ALM;
		SET WORK.Estresse_ALM;
		IF tpCenario='Pessimista' AND ValorMetrica < &nEstresse_ALM. then call symput('nEstresse_ALM',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_ALM;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse Consolidado';
		ValorMetrica = (&nEstresse_ALM.);
	RUN;

	/*Estresse TESOURARIA*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_TSA AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.tpCenario
						,(SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Cenario_Subcomissao' AND t1.tpCenario NOT IN ('HistoricoD','HistoricoDerivativos') AND t1.MesaOperacao ne '23 - ALM'
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_TSA;
		SET WORK.Estresse_TSA;
		IF tpCenario='Otimista' then call symput('nEstresse_TSA',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_TSA;
		SET WORK.Estresse_TSA;
		IF tpCenario='Pessimista' AND ValorMetrica < &nEstresse_TSA. then call symput('nEstresse_TSA',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_TSA;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse Consolidado';
		ValorMetrica = (&nEstresse_TSA.);
	RUN;

	%let nEstresse = 0;
	%let nEstresse_Banking = 0;
	%let nEstresse_Trading = 0;

	/*Estresse Consolidado*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_Consolidado AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.tpCenario,
	            (SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Cenario_Subcomissao' AND t1.tpCenario NOT IN ('HistoricoD','HistoricoDerivativos')
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Consolidado;
		SET WORK.Estresse_Consolidado;
		IF tpCenario='Otimista' then call symput('nEstresse',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Consolidado;
		SET WORK.Estresse_Consolidado;
		IF tpCenario='Pessimista' AND ValorMetrica < &nEstresse. then call symput('nEstresse',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_CA;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse - Consolidado - Limite Superior';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse.;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_CRC;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse - Consolidado - Limite Operacional';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse.;
	RUN;

	%put &nEstresse;

	/*Estresse Consolidado Banking*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Banking AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.tpCenario,
	            (SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Cenario_Subcomissao' AND t1.tpCenario NOT IN ('HistoricoD','HistoricoDerivativos')
		  		AND t1.TipoDeNegociacao = 'BANKING'
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Banking;
		SET WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Banking;
		IF tpCenario='Otimista' then call symput('nEstresse_Banking',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Banking;
		SET WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Banking;
		IF tpCenario='Pessimista' AND ValorMetrica < &nEstresse_Banking. then call symput('nEstresse_Banking',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	%put &nEstresse_Banking;

	/*Estresse Consolidado Trading*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Trading AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.tpCenario,
	            (SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Cenario_Subcomissao' AND t1.tpCenario NOT IN ('HistoricoD','HistoricoDerivativos')
		  		AND t1.TipoDeNegociacao = 'TRADING'
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Trading;
		SET WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Trading;
		IF tpCenario='Otimista' then call symput('nEstresse_Trading',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Trading;
		SET WORK.Estresse_Consolidado_Trading;
		IF tpCenario='Pessimista' AND ValorMetrica < &nEstresse_Trading. then call symput('nEstresse_Trading',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	%put &nEstresse_Trading;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Banking;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse - Consolidado - Banking';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_Banking.;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Trading;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse - Consolidado - Trading';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_Trading.;
	RUN;

	/* Limite Operacional */
	DATA WORK.Estresse_Banking_Operacional;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse - Consolidado - Banking Operacional';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_Banking.;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_Trading_Operacional;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse - Consolidado - Trading Operacional';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_Trading.;
	RUN;

%mend r05CalcEstresse;

	RUN;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE USD*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.nmCenario
	   					,t1.tpCenario
						,(SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Estresse_MesaVol' AND t1.tpCenario contains 'USD' AND t1.MesaOperacao eq '6 - VOLATILIDADE'
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD AS
	   SELECT t1.nmCenario,
	          t1.tpCenario,
	          t1.ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD t1
	      WHERE t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_1'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_7'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_01'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_02'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_03 '
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_10'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_11'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_08'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_08'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_4_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_04'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_05'
	      ORDER BY t1.ValorMetrica;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD;
		SET WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD;
		IF _N_ = 1 then call symput('nEstresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Mesa Volatilidade - Estresse Diário USD';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD.;
	RUN;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE IBOV*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.nmCenario
	   					,t1.tpCenario
						,(SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Estresse_MesaVol' AND t1.tpCenario contains 'IBOV' AND t1.MesaOperacao eq '6 - VOLATILIDADE'
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV AS
	   SELECT t1.nmCenario,
	          t1.tpCenario,
	          t1.ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV t1
	      WHERE t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'IBOV_1'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'IBOV_7'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_01'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_02'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_03 '
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_10'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_11'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_08'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_08'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_4_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_04'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_05'
	      ORDER BY t1.ValorMetrica;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV;
		SET WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV;
		IF _N_ = 1 then call symput('nEstresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Mesa Volatilidade - Estresse Diário IBOV';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV.;
	RUN;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE TOTAL*/
	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_TOTAL;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Mesa Volatilidade - Estresse Diário Total';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD. + &nEstresse_VOLATILIDADE_IBOV.;
	RUN;

%mend r06CalcEstresseMesa_2017;

		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_BSM_CUPOM_CAMBIAL.;

				t1.tpCenario NOT IN ('HistoricoD','HistoricoDerivativos') AND
				(t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'FICC' OR t1.MesaOperacao IN ('6 - VOLATILIDADE'))

				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Estresse IBOVESPA (Direcional) - FICC';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;

		%END;

						ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_IBOV_GESTOR.;
					RUN;
		%END;

%mend r06CalcEstresseMesa_2018;

/*Calcula Metricas Estress Mesa Vol*/;
%macro r06CalcEstresseMesaVol_2018 (dtDataBase, nDataBase);

	/*Inicia Variaveis*/
	%let nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_Vol 	= 0;
	%let nEstresse_VOL_USD_Vol 		= 0;
	%let nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_FICC 	= 0;
	%let nEstresse_VOL_USD_FICC 	= 0;
	%let nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR 	= 0;
	%let nEstresse_VOL_USD_GESTOR 	= 0;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE USD VOL*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.nmCenario
	   					,t1.tpCenario
						,(SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Estresse_MesaVol' AND t1.tpCenario contains 'USD' AND t1.MesaOperacao in ('6 - VOLATILIDADE','6 - FICC VOLATILIDADE')
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD AS
	   SELECT t1.nmCenario,
	          t1.tpCenario,
	          t1.ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD t1
	      WHERE t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_1'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_7'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_01'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_02'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_03 '
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_10'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_11'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_08'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_08'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_4_09'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_04'
			AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_05'
	      ORDER BY t1.ValorMetrica;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD;
		SET WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD;
		IF _N_ = 1 then call symput('nEstresse_VOL_USD_VOL',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse Vol USD - FICC VOLATILIDADE';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_USD_VOL.;
	RUN;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE USD FICC*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.nmCenario
	   					,t1.tpCenario
						,(SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'Estresse_MesaVol' AND t1.tpCenario contains 'USD' AND (t1.MesaOperacao Contains 'FICC' OR t1.MesaOperacao IN ('6 - VOLATILIDADE','6 - FICC VOLATILIDADE'))
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

		%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;

			DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Estresse Vol USD - FICC';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;

			DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC;
				SET WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC;
				IF _N_ = 1 then call symput('nEstresse_VOL_USD_FICC',ValorMetrica);
			RUN;

		%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
			PROC SQL;
			   CREATE TABLE WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC AS
			   SELECT t1.nmCenario,
			          t1.tpCenario,
			          t1.ValorMetrica
			      FROM WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC t1
			      WHERE t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_1'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_7'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_01'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_02'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_03 '
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_10'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_11'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_09'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_08'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_09'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_08'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_4_09'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_04'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_05'
			      ORDER BY t1.ValorMetrica;
			QUIT;

			DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC;
				SET WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC;
				IF _N_ = 1 then call symput('nEstresse_VOL_USD_FICC',ValorMetrica);
			RUN;

			DATA WORK.Estresse_VOLATILIDADE_USD_FICC;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Estresse Vol USD - FICC';
				ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_USD_FICC.;
			RUN;
		%END;

		/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE USD GESTOR*/

					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS 'USD_7'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_01'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_02'

				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Estresse Vol USD - FICC GESTOR';
				ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_USD_GESTOR.;
			RUN;
		%END;

					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_02'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_03 '
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_10'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_11'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_09'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_2_08'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_09'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_3_08'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_4_09'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_04'
					AND t1.tpCenario NOT CONTAINS '_6_05'
			      ORDER BY t1.ValorMetrica;
			QUIT;

			DATA WORK.Estresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR;
				SET WORK.Estresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR;
				IF _N_ = 1 then call symput('nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR',ValorMetrica);
			RUN;

			DATA WORK.Estresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Estresse Vol IBOV - FICC GESTOR';
				ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR.;
			RUN;
		%END;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE Total FICC*/
	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOL_Total_FICC;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse Vol Total - FICC';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_USD_FICC. + &nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_FICC.;
	RUN;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE Total VOL*/
	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOL_Total_VOL;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse Vol Total - FICC VOLATILIDADE';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_USD_VOL.+ &nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_VOL.;
	RUN;

	/*Estresse VOLATILIDADE Total GESTOR*/
	DATA WORK.Estresse_VOL_Total_GESTOR;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Estresse Vol Total - FICC GESTOR';
		ValorMetrica = &nEstresse_VOL_USD_GESTOR.+ &nEstresse_VOL_IBOV_GESTOR.;
	RUN;

%mend r06CalcEstresseMesaVol_2018;

/*Estresse Derivativo TJLP*/;
%macro r07EstresseDerivTJLP (dtDataBase, nDataBase);
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.tpCenario,
	            (SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIOS_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.tpCenario IN ('CUPOM_TJLP_+100','CUPOM_TJLP_-100') AND
				t1.ModalidadeSIC IN ('61 - Swap Fluxo de Caixa','64 - Swap Simples')
	      GROUP BY t1.tpCenario;
	QUIT;

	%if %f04checkConteudo (WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP) eq 1 %then %do;

		DATA WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP;
			SET WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP;
			IF tpCenario='CUPOM_TJLP_+100' then call symput('nEstresseDerivTJLP',ValorMetrica);
		RUN;

		DATA WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP;
			SET WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP;
			IF tpCenario='CUPOM_TJLP_-100' AND ValorMetrica < &nEstresseDerivTJLP. then call symput('nEstresseDerivTJLP',ValorMetrica);
		RUN;

		DATA WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP;
			DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
			Metrica = 'Estresse Derivativos TJLP';
			ValorMetrica = &nEstresseDerivTJLP.;
	/*		IF &nEstresseDerivTJLP.>0 then call symput('nEstresseDerivTJLP',0);*/
		RUN;

	%end;
	%else %do;

		DATA WORK.EstresseDerivTJLP;
			DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
			Metrica = 'Estresse Derivativos TJLP';
			ValorMetrica = 0;
		RUN;

	%end;

%mend r07EstresseDerivTJLP;

/*Derivativos TJLP - Exposicao PA/PP*/;
%macro r08DerivativosTJLP_AP (dtDataBase, nDataBase);

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.DerivativosTJLP_AP AS
	   SELECT t1.TipoContabil,
	            ABS(SUM(t2.Valor_Presente)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.TBPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	           INNER JOIN WORK.SENSFLUXO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t2 ON (t1.PosID = t2.PosId)
	      WHERE t1.Modalidade IN ('61 - Swap Fluxo de Caixa','64 - Swap Simples') AND
				t2.Fator_de_Risco IN ('Base Scenario','Base_Scenario') AND t1.IndexadorBV = 'TJLP'
	      GROUP BY t1.TipoContabil;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.DerivativosTJLP_A (DROP=TipoContabil);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Derivativos TJLP - Exposição Ponta Ativa';
		SET WORK.DerivativosTJLP_AP;
		IF TipoContabil='A' then output;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.DerivativosTJLP_P(DROP=TipoContabil);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Derivativos TJLP - Exposição Ponta Passiva';
		SET WORK.DerivativosTJLP_AP;
		IF TipoContabil='P' then output;
	RUN;

%mend r08DerivativosTJLP_AP;

/*Derivativos TJLP - Exposicao PA/PP*/;
%macro r09FlowBook (dtDataBase, nDataBase);

	%if &nDataBase. < &dtCorteOne. %then %do;

		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.FlowBook AS
		   SELECT t1.CarteiraGerencial,
		            (SUM(t1.Notional*t1.PositionUnits*t1.ContractSize)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
		      FROM BASERM.TBPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
		      WHERE t1.CarteiraGerencial IN ('35 - FLOW BOOK','35 - FICC FLOW BOOK')
				  	AND t1.Nome NOT CONTAINS 'BANVOR'
				 	AND t1.Modalidade	CONTAINS ('Eurobond')
					AND t1.Modalidade	CONTAINS ('Asset')
			GROUP BY t1.CarteiraGerencial;
		QUIT;

	%end;
	%else %do;

		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.FlowBook AS
		   SELECT t1.CarteiraGerencial,
		            (SUM(t1.Notional*t1.PositionUnits*t1.ContractSize)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
		      FROM PosOne.TBPOSICAOMOR_&nDataBase. t1
		      WHERE t1.CarteiraGerencial IN ('35 - FLOW BOOK','35 - FICC FLOW BOOK')
				  	AND t1.Nome NOT CONTAINS 'BANVOR'
				 	AND t1.Modalidade	CONTAINS ('Eurobond')
					AND t1.Modalidade	CONTAINS ('Asset')
			GROUP BY t1.CarteiraGerencial;
		QUIT;

	%end;

	DATA WORK.FlowBook (DROP=CarteiraGerencial);
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Flowbook (Eurobonds)';
		set WORK.FlowBook;
	RUN;

%mend r09FlowBook;

/*Capital Regulatorio*/;
%macro r10CapitalRegulatorio_2017 (dtDataBase, nDataBase, nVrDolar);

	/*Demais Parcelas Trading*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.DemaisParcTrading AS
	   SELECT t1.dtreferencia,
	          t1.vec_pjur_1,
	          t1.vpjur_2,
	          t1.vpjur_3,
	          t1.vpjur_4,
	          t1.vpcom,
	          t1.vpacs
	      FROM TBbatmen.TBBATIMENTODDR2011 t1
	      WHERE t1.dtreferencia = &dtDataBase.;
	QUIT;

	DATA _Null_;
	set WORK.DemaisParcTrading;
		call symput('nVec_pjur_1',vec_pjur_1);
		call symput('nVec_pjur_2',vpjur_2);
		call symput('nVec_pjur_3',vpjur_3);
		call symput('nVec_pjur_4',vpjur_4);
		call symput('nVpcom',vpcom);
		call symput('nVpacs',vpacs);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.DemaisParcTrading;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Demais Parcelas Trading';
		ValorMetrica = &nVec_pjur_1. + &nVec_pjur_2. + &nVec_pjur_3. + &nVec_pjur_4. + &nVpcom. + &nVpacs.;
	RUN;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.DemaisParcTrading;
		IF Metrica = 'Demais Parcelas Trading' then call symput('nDemParcTrading',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	/*RBAN*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.RBAN AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT (SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica,
	          t1.Origem,
	          t1.nmCenario,
	          t1.nuCenario
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'VOT_HS_Global_1d_1260d_RBAN' AND
				t1.TpNegociacao = 'BANKING' AND
				t1.nuCenario <= 500
	      GROUP BY t1.Origem,
	               t1.nmCenario,
	               t1.nuCenario
	      ORDER BY ValorMetrica;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.RBAN;
		set WORK.RBAN;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nRBAN',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.RBAN;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'RBAN';
		ValorMetrica = ABS(&nRBAN.*sqrt(21));
	RUN;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.RBAN;
		IF Metrica = 'RBAN' then call symput('nRBAN',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	/*	Exposicao Cambial em Modulo*/

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambModulo AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            ABS(SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32.2 AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM REGULAT.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM'
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambModulo AS
	   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica))/&nVrDolar. FORMAT=BEST32.2 AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambModulo t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambModulo;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial em Módulo';
		set WORK.ExpCambModulo;
	RUN;

	/*Exposicao Cambial NET*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNET AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            (SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32.2 AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM REGULAT.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM'
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNET AS
	   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32.2 AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambNET t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambNET;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Net';
		set WORK.ExpCambNET;
	RUN;

	/*EXP1 Cesta de Moedas*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.CestaMoedas AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            (SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32.2 AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM REGULAT.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM' AND
			(t1.FatorRisco = 'USD'
			OR t1.FatorRisco = 'EUR'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'GBP'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'JPY'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'CHF'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'Ouro'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'CAD')
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Exp1_CM AS
	   SELECT ABS(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) AS Exp1_CM
	      FROM WORK.CestaMoedas t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.Exp1_CM;
		call symput('nExp1_CM ',Exp1_CM);
	RUN;

	/*Exp1 Demais Moedas*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.DemaisMoedas AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            ABS(SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32.2 AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM REGULAT.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM' AND
			(t1.FatorRisco NE 'USD'
			AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'EUR'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'GBP'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'JPY'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'CHF'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'Ouro'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'CAD')
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Exp1_DM AS
	   SELECT (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) AS Exp1_DM
	      FROM WORK.DemaisMoedas t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.Exp1_DM;
		call symput('nExp1_DM ',Exp1_DM);
	RUN;

	/*Exp2*/
	data WORK.Exp2 (DROP=ValorMetrica FatorRisco);
		set WORK.CestaMoedas;
		IF ValorMetrica>0 then do ExC = ValorMetrica; ExV = 0; end;
		IF ValorMetrica<0 then do ExV = ValorMetrica; ExC = 0; end;
	run;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Exp2 AS
	   SELECT (SUM(t1.ExC)) AS Exp2_C,
			  (SUM(t1.ExV)) AS Exp2_V
	      FROM WORK.Exp2 t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.Exp2;
		IF Exp2_C<ABS(Exp2_V) then call symput('nExp2',Exp2_C);
		IF Exp2_C>ABS(Exp2_V) then call symput('nExp2',ABS(Exp2_V));
	RUN;

	/*Fator Pcam_H*/
	DATA _NULL_;
		set Cadastr.RWA_PARAMETROS&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF Multiplicador = 'Pcam_H' then call symput('nPcam_H',Valor);
	RUN;

	/*Fator F*/
	DATA _NULL_;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'FatorF' then call symput('nFatorF',OV);
	RUN;

	/*VTEC*/
	DATA _NULL_;
		set TBbatmen.TBBATIMENTODDR2011;
		IF dtreferencia=&dtDataBase. then call symput('nVTEC',1000*vtec);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambRWAcam_VTEC;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial RWAcam - VTEC';
/*		ValorMetrica = (&nExp1_CM.+&nExp1_DM.)+(&nExp2.*&nPcam_H.);*/
		ValorMetrica = &nVTEC.;

	RUN;

	DATA WORK.RWACAM;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'RWAcam';
/*		ValorMetrica = &nFatorF./100*((&nExp1_CM.+&nExp1_DM.)+(&nExp2.*&nPcam_H.));*/
		ValorMetrica = (&nFatorF./100)*(&nVTEC.);
	RUN;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.RWACAM;
		IF Metrica = 'RWAcam' then call symput('nRWAcam',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.CapitalRegulatorio;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Capital Regulatório Consolidado';
		ValorMetrica = &nDemParcTrading.*1000 + &nRBAN. + &nRWAcam.;
	RUN;

%mend r10CapitalRegulatorio_2017;

/*Capital Regulatorio*/;
%macro r10CapitalRegulatorioTD_2018 (dtDataBase, nDataBase, nVrDolar);

	DATA PJUR1;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'PJUR[1]' then call symput('nVec_pjur_1',OV);
	RUN;
	DATA PJUR2;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'VPJUR[2]' then call symput('nVec_pjur_2',OV);
	RUN;
	DATA PJUR3;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'VPJUR[3]' then call symput('nVec_pjur_3',OV);
	RUN;
	DATA PJUR4;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'VPJUR[4]' then call symput('nVec_pjur_4',OV);
	RUN;
	DATA VPCOM;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'VPCOM' then call symput('nVpcom',OV);
	RUN;
	DATA VPACS;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'VPACS' then call symput('nVpacs',OV);
	RUN;

	Data _null_;
		IF &nVec_pjur_1. =. then call symput('nVec_pjur_1',0);
		IF &nVec_pjur_2. =. then call symput('nVec_pjur_2',0);
		IF &nVec_pjur_3. =. then call symput('nVec_pjur_3',0);
		IF &nVec_pjur_4. =. then call symput('nVec_pjur_4',0);
		IF &nVpcom. =. then call symput('nVpcom',0);
		IF &nVpacs. =. then call symput('nVpacs',0);
	run;

	%put &nVec_pjur_1.;
	%put &nVec_pjur_2.;
	%put &nVec_pjur_3.;
	%put &nVec_pjur_4.;
	%put &nVpcom.;
	%put &nVpacs.;

	DATA WORK.DemaisParcTrading;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Demais Parcelas Trading';
		ValorMetrica = &nVec_pjur_1. + &nVec_pjur_2. + &nVec_pjur_3. + &nVec_pjur_4. + &nVpcom. + &nVpacs.;
	RUN;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.DemaisParcTrading;
		IF Metrica = 'Demais Parcelas Trading' then call symput('nDemParcTrading',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	/*RBAN*/
	/*	*/
	%IF &nDataBase.<= 20180209 %then %do;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.RBAN AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT (SUM(t1.vrCenario)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica,
	          t1.Origem,
	          t1.nmCenario,
	          t1.nuCenario
	      FROM WORK.TBCENARIO1D1260D_&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.nmCenario = 'VOT_HS_Global_1d_1260d_RBAN' AND
				t1.TpNegociacao = 'BANKING' AND
				t1.nuCenario <= 500
	      GROUP BY t1.Origem,
	               t1.nmCenario,
	               t1.nuCenario
	      ORDER BY ValorMetrica;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.RBAN;
		set WORK.RBAN;
		IF _N_=5 then call symput('nRBAN',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.RBAN;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'RBAN';
		ValorMetrica = ABS(&nRBAN.*sqrt(21));
	RUN;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.RBAN;
		IF Metrica = 'RBAN' then call symput('nRBAN',ValorMetrica);
		IF &nDataBase.<= 20180209 then call symput('nRBAN_Trading',ValorMetrica);
		IF &nDataBase.> 20180209 then call symput('nRBAN_Trading',0);
	RUN;

	%end;
	%else %do;

		%LET nRBAN_Trading = 0;

	%end;

	/*EXP1 Cesta de Moedas*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.CestaMoedas AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            (SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM REGULAT.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM' AND
			(t1.FatorRisco = 'USD'
			OR t1.FatorRisco = 'EUR'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'GBP'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'JPY'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'CHF'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'Ouro'
			  OR t1.FatorRisco = 'CAD')
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Exp1_CM AS
	   SELECT ABS(SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) AS Exp1_CM
	      FROM WORK.CestaMoedas t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.Exp1_CM;
		call symput('nExp1_CM ',Exp1_CM);
	RUN;

	/*Exp1 Demais Moedas*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.DemaisMoedas AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            ABS(SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM REGULAT.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM' AND
			(t1.FatorRisco NE 'USD'
			AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'EUR'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'GBP'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'JPY'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'CHF'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'Ouro'
			  AND t1.FatorRisco NE 'CAD')
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Exp1_DM AS
	   SELECT (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) AS Exp1_DM
	      FROM WORK.DemaisMoedas t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.Exp1_DM;
		call symput('nExp1_DM ',Exp1_DM);
	RUN;

	/*Exp2*/
	data WORK.Exp2 (DROP=ValorMetrica FatorRisco);
		set WORK.CestaMoedas;
		IF ValorMetrica>0 then do ExC = ValorMetrica; ExV = 0; end;
		IF ValorMetrica<0 then do ExV = ValorMetrica; ExC = 0; end;
	run;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.Exp2 AS
	   SELECT (SUM(t1.ExC)) AS Exp2_C,
			  (SUM(t1.ExV)) AS Exp2_V
	      FROM WORK.Exp2 t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.Exp2;
		IF Exp2_C<ABS(Exp2_V) then call symput('nExp2',Exp2_C);
		IF Exp2_C>ABS(Exp2_V) then call symput('nExp2',ABS(Exp2_V));
	RUN;

	/*Fator Pcam_H*/
	DATA _NULL_;
		set Cadastr.RWA_PARAMETROS&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF Multiplicador = 'Pcam_H' then call symput('nPcam_H',Valor);
	RUN;

	/*Fator F*/
	DATA _NULL_;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'FatorF' then call symput('nFatorF',OV);
	RUN;

	/*VTEC*/
	DATA _NULL_;
		set Regulat.DDR_SUMMARY&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		IF ItemDDR = 'VTEC' then call symput('nVTEC',OV);
	RUN;

/*	DATA _NULL_;*/
/*		set TBbatmen.TBBATIMENTODDR2011;*/
/*		IF dtreferencia=&dtDataBase. then call symput('nVTEC',1000*vtec);*/
/*	RUN;*/

	DATA WORK.ExpCambRWAcam_VTEC;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial RWAcam - VTEC';
/*		ValorMetrica = (&nExp1_CM.+&nExp1_DM.)+(&nExp2.*&nPcam_H.);*/
		ValorMetrica = &nVTEC./&nVrDolar.;

	RUN;

	DATA WORK.RWACAM;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'RWAcam';
/*		ValorMetrica = &nFatorF./100*((&nExp1_CM.+&nExp1_DM.)+(&nExp2.*&nPcam_H.));*/
		ValorMetrica = (&nFatorF./100)*(&nVTEC.);
	RUN;

	DATA _NULL_;
		set WORK.RWACAM;
		IF Metrica = 'RWAcam' then call symput('nRWAcam',ValorMetrica);
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.CapitalRegulatorio;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Capital Regulatório - Consolidado';
		IF &nDataBase.<= 20180209 then ValorMetrica = &nDemParcTrading.*1000 + &nRBAN_Trading. + &nRWAcam.;
		IF &nDataBase.> 20180209 then ValorMetrica = 0;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.CapitalRegulatorio_TRADING_CA;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Capital Regulatório - TRADING - CA';
		IF &nDataBase.<= 20180209 then ValorMetrica = 0;
		IF &nDataBase.> 20180209 then ValorMetrica = &nDemParcTrading. + &nRBAN_Trading. + &nRWAcam.;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.CapitalRegulatorio_TRADING_CCR;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Capital Regulatório - TRADING - CCR';
		ValorMetrica = &nDemParcTrading. + &nRBAN_Trading. + &nRWAcam.;
	RUN;

%mend r10CapitalRegulatorioTD_2018;

/*Exposiçao Cambial*/;
%macro r10ExpCambial_2018 (dtDataBase, nDataBase, nVrDolar);

	%if &nDataBase. < &dtCorteOne. %then %do;

		/*Exposiçao Cambial*/
		proc sort data=BaseRM.RWA_BACEN&stServidor._&nDataBase. out=RWA_BACEN;
			by PosID;
		run;
		proc sort data=BaseRM.TBPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. out=TBPOSICAO;
			by PosID;
		run;

		data WORK.RWAPOSICAO_Old&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
			merge 	RWA_BACEN(in=a)
					TBPOSICAO(in=b);
			by 		PosID;
		run;

		%if %f04checkConteudo (RWAOne.RWA_BACENMOR_&nDataBase.) NE 0 AND
			%f04checkConteudo (PosOne.TBPOSICAOMOR_&nDataBase.) NE 0 %then %do;

			/*ALGO ONE*/
			proc sort data=RWAOne.RWA_BACENMOR_&nDataBase. out=RWA_BACEN_One;
				by PosID;
			run;

			proc sort data=WORK.TBPOSICAOMOR_&nDataBase. out=TBPOSICAO_One;
				by PosID;
			run;

			data WORK.RWAPOSICAO_One&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
				merge 	RWA_BACEN_One(in=a)
						TBPOSICAO_One(in=b);
				by 		PosID;
			run;

		%end;

		%TrocaDemaisGrupos_ExpCambial(&stServidor., &nDataBase.);

	%end;
	%else %do;

		/*ALGO ONE*/
		proc sort data=RWAOne.RWA_BACENMOR_&nDataBase. out=RWA_BACEN_One;
			by PosID;
		run;

		proc sort data=WORK.TBPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. out=TBPOSICAO_One;
			by PosID;
		run;

		data WORK.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
			merge 	RWA_BACEN_One(in=a)
					TBPOSICAO_One(in=b);
			by 		PosID;
		run;

	%end;

	data WORK.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		set WORK.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase.;
		attrib ValorExposicaoTotal length=8 FORMAT=BEST32.2 INFORMAT=BEST32.2;
		RWA=UPCASE(RWA);

		ValorExposicaoTotal= Exposicao * PositionUnits * ContractSize ;

		if find(upcase(UnderlyingCurveIndex),"CDI") or find(upcase(UnderlyingCurveIndex),"SELIC") then do;
			if Factor > 1  and FatorRisco = "JJ1" and substr(GrupoContabilBacen,1,1) = "A" then
				GrupoContabilBacen = "P90 - OUTROS";
			else if Factor > 1  and FatorRisco = "JJ1" and substr(GrupoContabilBacen,1,1) = "P" then
				GrupoContabilBacen = "A90 - OUTROS";

		end;

		if substr(GrupoContabilBacen,1,1) = "A" then IdPosicao="C";
			else if substr(GrupoContabilBacen,1,1) = "P" then IdPosicao="V";
				else if substr(GrupoContabilBacen,1,1) = "D" and ValorExposicaoTotal < 0 then IdPosicao="V";
					else IdPosicao="C";

		if UPCASE(RWA) ne "PJUR1" AND PacsPais = "BR" then FatorRisco = "AA1";
		if UPCASE(RWA) ne "PJUR1" AND PacsPais = "US" then FatorRisco = "AA3";
		if UPCASE(RWA) eq "PCAM" AND FatorRisco = "USD-PTAX" then FatorRisco = "USD";
		if UPCASE(RWA) eq "PCAM"  AND Currency IN ("USD","USD-PTAX") AND COMPRESS(FatorRisco) eq "" then FatorRisco = "USD";
	Run;

	/*Exposição Cambial NET por Moeda*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambMoeda AS
	   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
	            (SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM'
	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNET AS
	   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambMoeda t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambNET;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Attrib Metrica format=$50.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Net';
		set WORK.ExpCambNET;

	RUN;

	/*	Exposicao Cambial em Modulo*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambModulo AS
	   SELECT
/*				t1.FatorRisco,*/
	            ((SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal))) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
	      WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM'
/*	      GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco*/
;
	QUIT;

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambModuloAux AS
	   SELECT   (SUM(ABS(t1.ValorMetrica))/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambModulo t1;
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambModulo;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial em Módulo - CA';
		ValorMetrica=ABS(ValorMetrica);
		set WORK.ExpCambModuloAux;
	RUN;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambModuloCCR;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial em Módulo - CCR';
		ValorMetrica=ABS(ValorMetrica);
		set WORK.ExpCambModuloAux;
	RUN;

	/*	Exposicao Cambial USD*/

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambUSD AS
	   SELECT  (t1.ValorMetrica)/&nVrDolar. FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambMoeda t1
	      WHERE  t1.FatorRisco = 'USD';
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambUSD;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial de USD';
		set WORK.ExpCambUSD;
	RUN;

	/*	Exposicao Cambial EUR*/

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambEUR AS
	   SELECT  (t1.ValorMetrica)/&nVrDolar. FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambMoeda t1
	      WHERE  t1.FatorRisco = 'EUR';
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambEUR;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial de EUR em USD';
		set WORK.ExpCambEUR;
	RUN;

	/*	Exposicao Cambial Outras Moedas*/

	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambOutros AS
	   SELECT  (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica))/&nVrDolar. FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	      FROM WORK.ExpCambMoeda t1
	    WHERE t1.FatorRisco not in ('USD','EUR');
	QUIT;

	DATA WORK.ExpCambOutros;
		DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
		Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Outras Moedas em USD';
		set WORK.ExpCambOutros;
	RUN;

	/*Exposição Cambial NET por Mesa*/
	PROC SQL;
	   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambMesa AS
	   SELECT DISTINCT t1.DtReferencia,
	          t1.RWA,
			  t1.FatorRisco,
			  t1.MesaOperacao,
		      (SUM(t1.ValorExposicaoTotal)/&nVrDolar.) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
	    FROM WORK.RWAPOSICAO&stServidor._&nDataBase. t1
		WHERE t1.RWA = 'PCAM'
		  group by t1.RWA,
				  t1.FatorRisco,
				  t1.MesaOperacao;
	QUIT;

	/*Exposição Cambial NET FICC */
		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFICC AS
		   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
					  t1.MesaOperacao,
		            ValorMetrica
		      FROM ExpCambMesa t1
			  WHERE t1.MesaOperacao IN ('2 - FICC RENDA FIXA','3 - FICC CAMBIO','9 - FICC OFFSHORE','6 - VOLATILIDADE','6 - FICC VOLATILIDADE','18 - FICC GESTOR')
			GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco,t1.MesaOperacao;
		QUIT;

		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFICC AS
		   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
		      FROM WORK.ExpCambNetFICC t1;
		QUIT;

		DATA WORK.ExpCambNetFICC;
			DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
			Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Net - FICC';
			set WORK.ExpCambNetFICC;
		RUN;

	/*	Exposicao Cambial em NET GESTOR*/
	PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR AS
		   SELECT  t1.FatorRisco,
		   t1.RWA,
		   t1.MesaOperacao,
		   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
		      FROM ExpCambMesa t1
			  WHERE t1.MesaOperacao eq '18 - FICC GESTOR'
			GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco,
		   t1.RWA,
		   t1.MesaOperacao;
		QUIT;

	%IF %f04checkConteudo(WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR) eq 0 %THEN
		%DO;
			DATA WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR ;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Net - FICC GESTOR';
				ValorMetrica = &nZero.;
				ATTRIB DtReferencia format = yymmdd10.;
			RUN;
		%END;
		%ELSE %DO;
			PROC SQL;
			   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR AS
			   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
			      FROM WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR t1;
			QUIT;

			DATA WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR;
				DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
				Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Net - FICC GESTOR';
				set WORK.ExpCambNetFiccGESTOR;
			RUN;
		%END;

		/*Exposição Cambial NET CAMBIO */
		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFiccCAMBIO AS
		   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
					t1.MesaOperacao,
		            t1.ValorMetrica
		      FROM ExpCambMesa t1
				WHERE	t1.MesaOperacao eq '3 - FICC CAMBIO'
			GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco,t1.MesaOperacao;
		QUIT;

		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFiccCAMBIO AS
		   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica
		      FROM WORK.ExpCambNetFiccCAMBIO t1;
		QUIT;

		DATA WORK.ExpCambNetFiccCAMBIO;
			DtReferencia = &dtDataBase.;
			Metrica = 'Exposição Cambial Net - FICC CAMBIO';
			set WORK.ExpCambNetFiccCAMBIO;
		RUN;

		/*Exposição Cambial NET FICC VOLATILIDADE */
		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetFiccVOL AS
		   SELECT   t1.FatorRisco,
					t1.MesaOperacao,
		            t1.ValorMetrica
		      FROM ExpCambMesa t1
				WHERE	t1.MesaOperacao in ('6 - VOLATILIDADE','6 - FICC VOLATILIDADE')
			GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco,t1.MesaOperacao;
		QUIT;

		            t1.ValorMetrica
		      FROM ExpCambMesa t1
				WHERE	t1.MesaOperacao contains 'BSM'
			GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco,t1.MesaOperacao;
		QUIT;

		PROC SQL;
		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetBSM AS
		   SELECT   (SUM(t1.ValorMetrica)) FORMAT=BEST32. AS ValorMetrica

			set WORK.ExpCambNetBSM;
		RUN;

		/*Exposição Cambial NET BSM CAIXA OFFSHORE */

			GROUP BY t1.FatorRisco,t1.MesaOperacao;

		/*Exposição Cambial NET NAO INFORMADO */

		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetNInfo AS

					t1.MesaOperacao,
		            t1.ValorMetrica
		      FROM ExpCambMesa t1
				WHERE	t1.MesaOperacao eq 'X - NAO INFORMADO'

		   CREATE TABLE WORK.ExpCambNetNInfo AS
